## 2026-10-03 -- E-068 slice 2: step 1a writes the claim block, checked before any spend (CUL-389, PR #319)
1. **Hypothesis:** Step 1a can state a claim and compose its test from the slice-1 slots, as a form rather than code. Code can check the form before any spend, with no change to any run while the flag is off.
2. **Result:**
   - **PR #319 OPEN** from master (worktree `.claude/worktrees/e068s2`, branch `feat/e068-s2-claim-card`, 3 commits).
   - Flag `orchestrator.claim_tests.enabled`: off by default, requires `config_direct_authoring`. Flag off: byte-identical.
   - **The claim block:** statement, kind (13 O-18 kinds), 1-3 slot tests (supported only if all pass) and/or `criteria_refs`, pass_if/fail_if (they decide nothing), rationale; or `tests: none` + `missing_block`.
   - **Checks before any spend:**
     - one shared 1a retry: for a refused claim, or for a `min_events` floor above (bars / longest horizon) x coins;
     - a refused claim after the retry fails the run; the power shortfall becomes a warning in `claim_power.yaml` and never stops the run;
     - `tests: none` writes a row in `campaign_record/test_requests.yaml`, then parks the run (kind `test`) or, without retired routing, pauses it;
     - regime selectors are effect-size only (`verdict_possible: false`, operator D3);
     - a gate before 1b checks queued extra cards; their `tests: none` is refused, because a queued card cannot be re-authored;
     - a 1a/1b mismatch is a warning in `claim_match.yaml`.
   - New files: `tools/claim_card.py`, `CLAIM_TESTS.md` (the slot menu, kept equal to the engine by a test). Also: SKILL IMPROVEMENT 10, D-066.
   - **Verification:**

     - Mutations: 37/37 killed.
     - Opus review, round 1: 7 should-fixes, all fixed (split-safe retry, stale 1a outputs set aside, a card cannot exempt itself, its own audit key, repeat checked first, no crash on malformed values, redundancy -> tests: none).
     - Opus review, round 2: 2 should-fixes, both fixed (a park before 1b could never clear; the retry would repeat the legacy split).
   - CI: first run failed only on the pinned hypothesis-design SKILL hash (declared text change); re-pinned in `fd642a41`; CI green on ubuntu + windows.

## 2026-10-04 -- E-068 slice 3 phase A: grading the claim tests after the backtests (CUL-393), STOPPED for the operator's nod
1. **Hypothesis:** the claim tests can be graded inside a run, information only, if (a) calibrations are stored with an exact key and a staleness check, (b) only signals the code can reproduce get a p-value, (c) grading has a time budget, and (d) it sits outside the grid.
2. **Result (phase A only, no code):**
   - Measured on run_065's saved bars: the vectorized shuffle-method grade takes 2.5-4.9 s per test x variant at N=1000 (0.5-2.6 s at N=199); effect sizes alone take 0.01 s. Donchian(14) got no p-value (excluded from the timing).
   - Driving the real AdvancedStrategy (forecast_size_probe pattern) on run_065 `base` reproduces the saved forecast within 5e-7, at 2.35 ms/bar: fine as a check, too slow for a 400-sim gate (~38 h per cell).
   - CUL-387 root cause found: the engine's data_buffer keeps only required_bars + 100 bars (main_strategy.py:76-80), so the Keltner EWM restarts every bar; claim_tests' copy uses the full history.
   - calibration_for (claim_tests.py:1180-1196) ignores selector, outcome kind, baseline, number of fakes, alpha, layout and the code hash. Under the operator's fixed rule, the published run_065 grade (N=1000 against a gate run at N=199) would read "verdict pending".
   - Design questions (a)-(f) answered in chat; plan in tasks/todo.md.

   - What it does:
     - `tools/claim_measure.py` measures the run's own card tests on each variant backtested in THIS attempt;
     - it writes `artifacts/variants/<vid>/claim_test.yaml` and `artifacts/claim_status.yaml` (measured / not_measured + reason);

     - not a grid criterion; errors are recorded and never raised.

Operator reworded it 2026-10-17: a line dies when no claim-built variant has "whole-test Sharpe after costs > 0 AND chained total return > 0 on its fold". No code.
   - A (does the Sharpe bar pass a losing strategy): yes in arithmetic. run_096's LTC variant: mean daily +0.672%, daily Sharpe 0.057, derived daily stdev 11.9%, so the volatility drag (half the variance, 0.71% a day) exceeds the mean and the chained return is -23% over 729 days (runs/run_096/artifacts/profit_bars_evaluation.yaml). Sharpe = mean over sample stdev of chained daily simple returns, times the square root of 365 (tools/portfolio_whole_test.py:819-840); the leverage comes from allocation = forecast / 10 up to 200% (execution/forecast_manager.py:19, :70). Scan of every bars file: 49 variants in 23 runs carry a Sharpe row, 15 pass it, 4 have a negative total return (run_094 ada_altcoin_asset and uni_defi_asset, run_096 asset_LTCUSDT and base) and all 4 fail the drawdown bar; 0 pass overall. A positive Sharpe with negative compounding needs a daily stdev above about 3.2% once the 0.05%-a-day bar holds, and at that volatility the 20% drawdown bar is out of reach. Ruling (c): no new branch-3 bar; the ledger effect (a positive-Sharpe loser raises the deflated-Sharpe benchmark, tools/deflate_sharpe.py:379-479) is immaterial; `whole_test_sharpe_positive` reaches the grid only through a pre-registered brief (config/criterion_menu.yaml:123; absent from step 1a's skill since D-143, the three closed criterion questions). Operator approved (c).
   - B (process state, measured): 25 registered worktrees plus 2 orphan folders, about 530 MB each (about 13 GB); disk 476 GB with 4.3 GB free at close; local_data 18.2 GB; runs 2.5 GB; operator checkout `c4/flag-set` holds 11,621 status entries, 11,547 of them staged and never committed (run folders from run_061 on), 16 untracked; master tracks run_0001..run_060; the main venv folder has no python.exe (the junction repeat of 2026-09-28); 22 of 23 worktree branches merged, `docs/session-2026-10-10-2b` not (two commits, session logs 2026-10-10/11). Root causes: the venv junction plus old commits that tracked venv, runtime records staged but never committed, no worktree lifecycle. Ruling: a dedicated process-review agent, diagnose and propose only, nothing deleted before the operator approves its order; prompt delivered. Operator approved. Venv repair comes first (operator runs `python -m venv --upgrade venv`, then ensurepip, then an import check; the implementer verifies with tests).

The direction session proposed a long-only vehicle (clip the forecast at zero) with a control brief, twice; the operator rejected both as an exclusion rule fitted to one observation (D-051 spirit: graded forecasts, no on/off source). Re-ruling: the trade lens ran the operator's loop (confirm, name a mechanism, run its query) exactly once and stopped because step 3 of the method says "If it fails, drop the candidate" (SKILL.md:44-47). Fix = four sentences in the analyst skill, docs only, a fix of an existing link like D-139 (the decide-next to pre-registration link): step 3 continues to the next mechanism while the behaviour holds; a claim the memory view shows confirmed on this strategy is settled and the new claim is one reason it holds, `vehicle: []`, confirmed on the next fold; a config change is a vehicle only when its mechanism is confirmed and keeps the forecast graded (a signal parameter or a detector regime condition, never a side excluded, a clip at zero, a threshold switch or a rule fitted to one observation); one worked example from run_096. Of the operator's five example mechanisms, three are testable with today's selectors (continuation after a large up-move, forecast mis-scaled on the negative side, trend via a past-return quantile), a volatility regime is not (no trailing-volatility selector, regime labels all `unknown`), market drift is evidence not a claim, and a cost split does not exist (funding not modelled on Kraken). Measurement without spending a child: the smoke CLI `python tools/analyst_session.py --run <copy of run_096 outside runs/> --lens <lens>` runs one real session on a copy (36 MB), about $1 per lens, nothing enters the queue or the ledger. Consequence stated, not decided: a line now takes at least three children (behaviour, mechanism, vehicle). The fold-B re-run of run_096's host is not decided. Operator approved the skill fix and the smoke sessions.
   - D (unread feeds): the engine registers every feed in `extra_feeds` and uses `required_feeds` only to refuse a missing one (trading-bot/core/backtester.py:238-269; `required = feed_name in required_feeds` at :268), so Fear & Greed is fetched, merged and offered to the analyst's `list_columns` on strategies that never read it.
